============================================================
SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Portfolio workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores generated/cache/runtime/private files:
- .git
- node_modules
- .next
- .next.*
- out
- dist
- build
- .cache
- .turbo
- .npm
- .pnpm-store
- .yarn
- .DS_Store
- next-env.d.ts
- *.tsbuildinfo
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.zip
- .env and .env*.local

Binary image, PDF, and archive files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

     1	import type { Metadata, Viewport } from "next";

     7	  subsets: ["latin"],
     8	  style: "italic",
     9	  variable: "--font-eb-garamond",

   649	function PhonePreview({
   650	  title,
   651	  metric,
   652	  variant,
   653	  href,
   654	  screenImageSrc,
   655	  screenImageAlt,
   656	}: {
   657	  href?: string;
   658	  title: string;
   659	  metric: string;
   660	  variant: "commerce" | "finance";
   661	  screenImageSrc?: string;
   662	  screenImageAlt?: string;
   663	}) {
   664	  const className = `phone-shell phone-shell-${variant}${
   665	    screenImageSrc ? " phone-shell-capture" : ""
   666	  }${href ? " phone-shell-link" : ""}`;
   667	  const content = screenImageSrc ? (
   668	    <Image
   669	      alt={screenImageAlt ?? `${title} app screen`}
   670	      className="phone-framed-image"
   671	      fill
   672	      sizes="245px"
   673	      src={screenImageSrc}
   674	    />
   675	  ) : (
   676	    <>
   677	      <div className="phone-speaker" />
   678	      <div className="phone-screen">
   679	        <div className="phone-status">
   680	          <span>{title}</span>
   681	          <span>5G</span>
   682	        </div>
   683	        <div className="phone-chart">
   684	          <div />
   685	          <div />
   686	          <div />
   687	          <div />
   688	        </div>
   689	        <div className="phone-cardline wide" />
   690	        <div className="phone-cardline" />
   691	        <div className="phone-grid">
   692	          <span />
   693	          <span />
   694	          <span />
   695	          <span />
   696	        </div>
   697	        <div className="phone-cta">{metric}</div>
   698	      </div>
   699	    </>
   700	  );
   701	
   702	  if (href) {
   703	    return (
   704	      <a
   705	        aria-label={`Open ${title} case study`}
   706	        className={className}
   707	        href={href}
   708	      >
   709	        {content}
   710	      </a>
   711	    );
   712	  }
   713	
   714	  return <div className={className}>{content}</div>;
   715	}

   732	  );
   733	}

    74	function getCaseStudyRowScrollOffset() {
    75	  const isWideLandscape = window.matchMedia(
    76	    "(min-width: 1024px) and (orientation: landscape)",
    77	  ).matches;
    78	  const isPortrait = window.matchMedia("(orientation: portrait)").matches;
    79	
    80	  if (isWideLandscape) {
    81	    return Math.max(18, Math.min(44, window.innerHeight * 0.035));
    82	  }
    83	
    84	  if (isPortrait) {
    85	    return Math.max(14, Math.min(36, window.innerHeight * 0.03));
    86	  }
    87	
    88	  return Math.max(16, Math.min(40, window.innerHeight * 0.0325));
    89	}
    90	
    91	function scrollCaseStudyRowIntoView(caseStudyId: string) {
    92	  const targetCard = document.getElementById(caseStudyId);
    93	
    94	  if (!targetCard) {
    95	    return;
    96	  }
    97	
    98	  const caseGrid = targetCard.closest<HTMLElement>(".case-grid");
    99	  const targetTop = targetCard.getBoundingClientRect().top;
   100	  const rowCards = caseGrid
   101	    ? Array.from(caseGrid.children).filter((child): child is HTMLElement => {
   102	        if (!(child instanceof HTMLElement)) {
   103	          return false;
   104	        }
   105	
   106	        const cardTop = child.getBoundingClientRect().top;
   107	
   108	        return Math.abs(cardTop - targetTop) <= 8;
   109	      })
   110	    : [targetCard];
   111	  const rowTop = Math.min(
   112	    ...rowCards.map((card) => card.getBoundingClientRect().top),
   113	  );
   114	
   115	  window.scrollTo({
   116	    behavior: "smooth",
   117	    top: window.scrollY + rowTop - getCaseStudyRowScrollOffset(),
   118	  });
   119	
   120	  window.history.replaceState(null, "", `#${caseStudyId}`);
   121	}

   307	function getPlatformListGridTemplate(columnCount: number) {
   308	  const columns = ["max-content", "1px"];
   309	
   310	  Array.from({ length: columnCount }).forEach((_, index) => {
   311	    columns.push("max-content");
   312	
   313	    if (index < columnCount - 1) {
   314	      columns.push("1px");
   315	    }
   316	  });
   317	
   318	  return columns.join(" ");
   319	}

   351	function PlatformLabelDivider({
   352	  className,
   353	  gridColumn,
   354	  gridRow,
   355	  kind,
   356	}: {
   357	  className?: string;
   358	  gridColumn?: number;

   385	function PlatformLabelSlots({
   386	  labels,
   387	  row,
   388	}: {

   403	function PlatformColumnDividers({
   404	  columnCount,
   405	  gridRow,
   406	  partial,
   407	  startIndex = 0,
   408	}: {
   409	  columnCount: number;
   410	  gridRow: number | string;
   411	  partial?: boolean;
   412	  startIndex?: number;
   413	}) {
   414	  return Array.from({ length: Math.max(0, columnCount - 1 - startIndex) }).map(
   415	    (_, index) => {
   416	      const dividerIndex = startIndex + index;
   417	
   418	      return (
   419	        <PlatformLabelDivider
   420	          className={
   421	            partial
   422	              ? "case-card-title-platform-partial-divider"
   423	              : undefined
   424	          }
   425	          gridColumn={getPlatformDividerGridColumn(dividerIndex)}
   426	          gridRow={gridRow}
   427	          key={`divider-${gridRow}-${dividerIndex}`}
   428	          kind="item"
   429	        />
   430	      );
   431	    },
   432	  );
   433	}

   451	        fill="none"
   452	        stroke="#000"
   453	        strokeLinecap="round"
   454	        strokeWidth="2.4"
   455	      />
   456	      <path
   457	        d="M5.5 14a6.5 6.5 0 0 1 13 0v1H5.5z"
   458	        fill="#f3fff7"
   459	        stroke="#000"
   460	        strokeLinecap="round"
   461	        strokeLinejoin="round"
   462	        strokeWidth="2.4"
   463	      />
   464	      <circle cx="9.7" cy="11.7" fill="#000" r="0.56" />
   465	      <circle cx="14.3" cy="11.7" fill="#000" r="0.56" />
   466	    </svg>
   467	  );
   468	}
   469	
   470	function ApplePlatformMark() {

   472	    <svg
   473	      aria-hidden
   474	      className="case-card-title-platform-mark case-card-title-platform-mark-apple"
   475	      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
   476	    >
   477	      <path
   478	        d="M12 6.5V3.4c0-.9.7-1.6 1.6-1.6h.3"
   479	        fill="none"
   480	        stroke="#000"
   481	        strokeLinecap="round"
   482	        strokeLinejoin="round"
   483	        strokeWidth="4"
   484	      />
   485	      <path
   486	        d="M17.8 21.1c-1.1.7-2.1.8-3 .3-1.7-.8-3.1-.8-4.8 0-1 .5-2 .4-3-.3C4.8 19.5 3.2 15.2 3.4 11.6c.2-3.2 2.4-5.4 5-5.4 1.4 0 2.4.7 3.2.7.7 0 1.8-.7 3.2-.7 2.2 0 4.1 1.5 4.8 3.7-1.6.7-2.4 1.9-2.4 3.5 0 1.8 1 3.1 2.6 3.7-.5 1.6-1.2 3-2 4Z"
   487	        fill="#f3fff7"
   488	        stroke="#000"
   489	        strokeLinecap="round"
   490	        strokeLinejoin="round"
   491	        strokeWidth="4"
   492	      />
   493	    </svg>
   494	  );
   495	}

   497	function PlatformLogoMark({ platform }: { platform: MobilePlatform }) {
   498	  return platform === "android" ? (
   499	    <AndroidPlatformMark />
   500	  ) : (
   501	    <ApplePlatformMark />
   502	  );
   503	}

   505	function FactorDeviceMark({ device }: { device: "phone" | "tablet" }) {
   506	  return device === "phone" ? (
   507	    <PhoneMark mobilePlatforms={[]} />
   508	  ) : (
   509	    <TabletMark showPlatformIcon={false} />
   510	  );
   511	}
   512	
   513	export function ProjectDeviceStack({
   514	  assetGap,
   515	  color,
   516	  hasTablet,
   517	  hasWeb,
   518	  deviceLabels,
   519	  mobilePlatforms,
   520	  responsiveAssetGap,
   521	  showPlatformLabels,
   522	  webIconScale,
   523	}: {
   524	  assetGap?: string;
   525	  color: string;
   526	  deviceLabels?: string[];
   527	  hasTablet?: boolean;
   528	  hasWeb?: boolean;
   529	  mobilePlatforms: MobilePlatform[];
   530	  responsiveAssetGap?: ResponsiveAssetGap;
   531	  showPlatformLabels?: boolean;
   532	  webIconScale?: number;
   533	}) {
   534	  const stackRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null);
   535	  const [measuredAssetGap, setMeasuredAssetGap] = useState<string | null>(null);
   536	  const mobilePlatformKey = mobilePlatforms.join(",");
   537	  const stackStyle: CSSProperties & {
   538	    "--project-device-stack-gap"?: string;
   539	  } = {
   540	    color,
   541	  };
   542	  const stackGap = measuredAssetGap ?? assetGap;
   543	  const deviceLabelKey = deviceLabels?.join(",") ?? "";
   544	
   545	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   546	    if (!responsiveAssetGap) {
   547	      setMeasuredAssetGap(null);
   548	      return;
   549	    }
   550	
   551	    const stack = stackRef.current;
   552	    const measurementTarget = stack?.parentElement ?? stack;
   553	
   554	    if (!measurementTarget) {
   555	      return;
   556	    }
   557	
   558	    let frame = 0;
   559	    const updateGap = () => {
   560	      if (frame) {
   561	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   562	      }
   563	
   564	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   565	        const laneWidth = measurementTarget.getBoundingClientRect().width;
   566	        const activeRange = Math.max(
   567	          1,
   568	          responsiveAssetGap.wideLanePx - responsiveAssetGap.tightLanePx,
   569	        );
   570	        const progress = Math.min(
   571	          1,
   572	          Math.max(
   573	            0,
   574	            (laneWidth - responsiveAssetGap.tightLanePx) / activeRange,
   575	          ),
   576	        );
   577	        const nextGap =
   578	          responsiveAssetGap.minPx +
   579	          (responsiveAssetGap.maxPx - responsiveAssetGap.minPx) * progress;
   580	        const nextGapValue = `${nextGap.toFixed(2)}px`;
   581	
   582	        setMeasuredAssetGap((currentGap) =>
   583	          currentGap === nextGapValue ? currentGap : nextGapValue,
   584	        );
   585	      });
   586	    };
   587	
   588	    updateGap();
   589	
   590	    const resizeObserver =
   591	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
   592	        ? null
   593	        : new ResizeObserver(updateGap);
   594	
   595	    resizeObserver?.observe(measurementTarget);
   596	    window.addEventListener("resize", updateGap);
   597	
   598	    return () => {
   599	      if (frame) {
   600	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   601	      }
   602	
   603	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
   604	      window.removeEventListener("resize", updateGap);
   605	    };
   606	  }, [
   607	    responsiveAssetGap?.maxPx,
   608	    responsiveAssetGap?.minPx,
   609	    responsiveAssetGap?.tightLanePx,
   610	    responsiveAssetGap?.wideLanePx,
   611	  ]);
   612	
   613	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   614	    if (!showPlatformLabels) {
   615	      return;
   616	    }
   617	
   618	    const stack = stackRef.current;
   619	    const rowDivider = stack?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
   620	      ".case-card-title-platform-row-divider",
   621	    );
   622	
   623	    if (!stack || !rowDivider) {
   624	      return;
   625	    }
   626	
   627	    let frame = 0;
   628	    const updateRowDivider = () => {
   629	      if (frame) {
   630	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   631	      }
   632	
   633	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   634	        const platformHeading = stack.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
   635	          ".case-card-title-platform-heading-platform",
   636	        );
   637	        const deviceHeading = stack.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
   638	          ".case-card-title-platform-heading-factor",
   639	        );
   640	        const verticalDividers = Array.from(
   641	          stack.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
   642	            ".case-card-title-platform-divider",
   643	          ),
   644	        );
   645	
   646	        if (
   647	          !platformHeading ||
   648	          !deviceHeading ||
   649	          verticalDividers.length === 0
   650	        ) {
   651	          stack.removeAttribute("data-platform-row-divider-ready");
   652	          return;
   653	        }
   654	
   655	        const stackRect = stack.getBoundingClientRect();
   656	        const platformRect = platformHeading.getBoundingClientRect();
   657	        const deviceRect = deviceHeading.getBoundingClientRect();
   658	        const dividerRects = verticalDividers.map((divider) =>
   659	          divider.getBoundingClientRect(),
   660	        );
   661	        const leftmostDividerCenter = Math.min(
   662	          ...dividerRects.map((rect) => rect.left + rect.width / 2),
   663	        );
   664	        const rightmostDividerCenter = Math.max(
   665	          ...dividerRects.map((rect) => rect.left + rect.width / 2),
   666	        );
   667	        const dividerStartCenter = deviceRect.left + deviceRect.width / 2;
   668	        const dividerOverhang = Math.max(
   669	          0,
   670	          leftmostDividerCenter - dividerStartCenter,
   671	        );
   672	        const dividerEndCenter = rightmostDividerCenter + dividerOverhang;
   673	        const dividerStart = dividerStartCenter - stackRect.left;
   674	        const dividerEnd = dividerEndCenter - stackRect.left;
   675	        const dividerTop =
   676	          (platformRect.top +
   677	            platformRect.height / 2 +
   678	            deviceRect.top +
   679	            deviceRect.height / 2) /
   680	            2 -
   681	          stackRect.top;
   682	        const dividerWidth = Math.max(0, dividerEnd - dividerStart);
   683	
   684	        rowDivider.style.setProperty(
   685	          "--case-platform-row-divider-left",
   686	          `${dividerStart.toFixed(2)}px`,
   687	        );
   688	        rowDivider.style.setProperty(
   689	          "--case-platform-row-divider-top",
   690	          `${dividerTop.toFixed(2)}px`,
   691	        );
   692	        rowDivider.style.setProperty(
   693	          "--case-platform-row-divider-width",
   694	          `${dividerWidth.toFixed(2)}px`,
   695	        );
   696	        stack.toggleAttribute(
   697	          "data-platform-row-divider-ready",
   698	          dividerWidth > 0,
   699	        );
   700	      });
   701	    };
   702	
   703	    updateRowDivider();
   704	    document.fonts?.ready.then(updateRowDivider);
   705	
   706	    const resizeObserver =
   707	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
   708	        ? null
   709	        : new ResizeObserver(updateRowDivider);
   710	
   711	    resizeObserver?.observe(stack);
   712	    window.addEventListener("resize", updateRowDivider);
   713	
   714	    return () => {
   715	      if (frame) {
   716	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   717	      }
   718	
   719	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
   720	      window.removeEventListener("resize", updateRowDivider);
   721	    };
   722	  }, [
   723	    deviceLabelKey,
   724	    hasTablet,
   725	    hasWeb,
   726	    mobilePlatformKey,
   727	    showPlatformLabels,
   728	    stackGap,
   729	  ]);
   730	
   731	  if (stackGap) {
   732	    stackStyle["--project-device-stack-gap"] = stackGap;
   733	  }
   734	
   735	  if (showPlatformLabels) {
   736	    const platforms: Array<MobilePlatform | "web"> = [];
   737	    const factorDevices: Array<"phone" | "tablet"> = [];
   738	
   739	    if (mobilePlatforms.includes("android") || hasTablet) {
   740	      platforms.push("android");
   741	    }
   742	
   743	    if (mobilePlatforms.includes("apple")) {
   744	      platforms.push("apple");
   745	    }
   746	
   747	    if (hasWeb) {
   748	      platforms.push("web");
   749	    }
   750	
   751	    if (mobilePlatforms.length > 0) {
   752	      factorDevices.push("phone");
   753	    }
   754	
   755	    if (hasTablet) {
   756	      factorDevices.push("tablet");
   757	    }
   758	
   759	    const platformLabels = platforms.map((platform) =>
   760	      platform === "web" ? "WEB" : platform === "android" ? "AND" : "IOS",
   761	    );
   762	    const factorDeviceLabels =
   763	      deviceLabels ??
   764	      factorDevices.map((device) =>
   765	        device === "phone" ? "PHONE" : "TABLET",
   766	      );
   767	    const labelColumnCount = Math.max(
   768	      platformLabels.length,
   769	      factorDeviceLabels.length,
   770	      1,
   771	    );
   772	    const dividerGridRow = factorDeviceLabels.length > 0 ? "1 / 3" : 1;
   773	    const spanningItemDividerCount =
   774	      factorDeviceLabels.length > platformLabels.length
   775	        ? platformLabels.length
   776	        : Math.max(0, platformLabels.length - 1);
   777	    const platformListStyle = {
   778	      ...stackStyle,
   779	      gridTemplateColumns: getPlatformListGridTemplate(labelColumnCount),
   780	    };
   781	
   782	    return (
   783	      <span
   784	        className="carousel-button-project case-card-title-platform-list"
   785	        ref={stackRef}
   786	        style={platformListStyle}
   787	      >
   788	        <span className="case-card-title-platform-heading case-card-title-platform-heading-platform">
   789	          PLATFORM
   790	        </span>
   791	        <PlatformLabelDivider
   792	          gridColumn={2}
   793	          gridRow={dividerGridRow}
   794	          kind="grid"
   795	        />
   796	        <PlatformLabelSlots labels={platformLabels} row={1} />
   797	        <PlatformColumnDividers
   798	          columnCount={spanningItemDividerCount + 1}
   799	          gridRow={dividerGridRow}
   800	        />
   801	        {factorDeviceLabels.length > 0 ? (
   802	          <>
   803	            <span className="case-card-title-platform-heading case-card-title-platform-heading-factor">
   804	              DEVICE
   805	            </span>
   806	            <PlatformLabelSlots labels={factorDeviceLabels} row={2} />
   807	            <PlatformColumnDividers
   808	              columnCount={factorDeviceLabels.length}
   809	              gridRow={2}
   810	              partial
   811	              startIndex={spanningItemDividerCount}
   812	            />
   813	            <span
   814	              aria-hidden="true"
   815	              className="case-card-title-platform-row-divider"
   816	            />
   817	          </>
   818	        ) : null}
   819	      </span>
   820	    );
   821	  }
   822	
   823	  return (
   824	    <span className="carousel-button-project" ref={stackRef} style={stackStyle}>
   825	      {mobilePlatforms.map((platform) => (
   826	        <PhoneMark key={platform} mobilePlatforms={[platform]} />
   827	      ))}
   828	      {hasTablet ? <TabletMark /> : null}
   829	      {hasWeb ? <WebMark scale={webIconScale} /> : null}
   830	    </span>
   831	  );
   832	}
   833	
   834	function CarouselArrowMark() {
   835	  return (
   836	    <img
   837	      alt=""
   838	      aria-hidden
   839	      className="carousel-button-arrow-mark"
   840	      draggable={false}
   841	      style={{
   842	        display: "block",
   843	        height: "100%",
   844	        verticalAlign: "top",
   845	        width: "100%",
   846	      }}
   847	      src="/images/carousel-arrow.svg"
   848	    />
   849	  );
   850	}
   851	
   852	export function ProjectCarouselButton() {
   853	  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0);
   854	  const projectButtonRef = useRef<HTMLAnchorElement>(null);
   855	  const activeProject = projectLinks[activeIndex];
   856	
   857	  const handleArrowClick = (event: MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) => {
   858	    event.preventDefault();
   859	    scrollCaseStudyRowIntoView(activeProject.caseStudyId);
   860	  };
   861	
   862	  useEffect(() => {
   863	    const interval = window.setInterval(() => {
   864	      setActiveIndex((currentIndex) => (currentIndex + 1) % projectLinks.length);
   865	    }, 5000);
   866	
   867	    return () => window.clearInterval(interval);
   868	  }, []);
   869	
   870	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   871	    let frame = 0;
   872	
   873	    const syncHeroActionGeometry = () => {
   874	      if (frame) {
   875	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   876	      }
   877	
   878	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   879	        const heroCopy = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-copy");
   880	        const heroActions =
   881	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-actions");
   882	        const proofPanel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".proof-panel");
   883	        const stackRow = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".stack-row");
   884	        const heroVisual = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-visual");
   885	        const phoneStage = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".phone-stage");
   886	        const navBar = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".nav-bar");
   887	        const profilePhoto =
   888	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".profile-photo");
   889	        const socialButton =
   890	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-social-button");
   891	        const visualFrameSelectors = [
   892	          ".profile-photo",
   893	          ".phone-shell-commerce",
   894	          ".phone-shell-finance",
   895	          ".tablet-shell",
   896	        ];
   897	
   898	        if (proofPanel && profilePhoto) {
   899	          const proofRect = proofPanel.getBoundingClientRect();
   900	          const profileRect = profilePhoto.getBoundingClientRect();
   901	          const proofWidth = profileRect.right - proofRect.left;
   902	
   903	          if (proofWidth > 0) {
   904	            [heroCopy, proofPanel, stackRow].forEach((element) => {
   905	              element?.style.setProperty(
   906	                "--hero-proof-landscape-width",
   907	                `${proofWidth}px`,
   908	              );
   909	            });
   910	          }
   911	        }
   912	
   913	        if (heroVisual && navBar && stackRow) {
   914	          const isTwoColumnHero = window.matchMedia(
   915	            "(min-width: 1041px), (min-width: 981px) and (orientation: landscape)",
   916	          ).matches;
   917	
   918	          if (!isTwoColumnHero) {
   919	            heroVisual.style.removeProperty("--hero-visual-center-offset");
   920	          } else {
   921	            const isCompactLandscapeHero = window.matchMedia(
   922	              "(min-width: 981px) and (max-width: 1023px) and (orientation: landscape)",
   923	            ).matches;
   924	            const heroVisualScale =
   925	              heroVisual.offsetWidth > 0
   926	                ? heroVisual.getBoundingClientRect().width / heroVisual.offsetWidth
   927	                : 1;
   928	            const deviceStageSnap = isCompactLandscapeHero
   929	              ? (Number.parseFloat(
   930	                  window
   931	                    .getComputedStyle(phoneStage ?? heroVisual)
   932	                    .getPropertyValue("--hero-device-stage-snap"),
   933	                ) || 0) * heroVisualScale
   934	              : 0;
   935	            const visualRects = visualFrameSelectors
   936	              .map((selector) => ({
   937	                element: document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(selector),
   938	                selector,
   939	              }))
   940	              .filter(
   941	                (
   942	                  entry,
   943	                ): entry is { element: HTMLElement; selector: string } =>
   944	                  Boolean(entry.element),
   945	              )
   946	              .map(({ element, selector }) => {
   947	                const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
   948	                const snapAdjustment =
   949	                  selector === ".profile-photo" ? 0 : deviceStageSnap;
   950	
   951	                return {
   952	                  bottom: rect.bottom - snapAdjustment,
   953	                  top: rect.top - snapAdjustment,
   954	                };
   955	              });
   956	
   957	            if (visualRects.length > 0) {
   958	              const navRect = navBar.getBoundingClientRect();
   959	              const stackRect = stackRow.getBoundingClientRect();
   960	              const visualTop = Math.min(
   961	                ...visualRects.map((rect) => rect.top),
   962	              );
   963	              const visualBottom = Math.max(
   964	                ...visualRects.map((rect) => rect.bottom),
   965	              );
   966	              const currentOffset =
   967	                Number.parseFloat(
   968	                  window
   969	                    .getComputedStyle(heroVisual)
   970	                    .getPropertyValue("--hero-visual-center-offset"),
   971	                ) || 0;
   972	              const visualCenterWithoutOffset =
   973	                (visualTop + visualBottom) / 2 - currentOffset;
   974	              const targetCenter = (navRect.bottom + stackRect.top) / 2;
   975	              const nextOffset = targetCenter - visualCenterWithoutOffset;
   976	
   977	              heroVisual.style.setProperty(
   978	                "--hero-visual-center-offset",
   979	                `${nextOffset}px`,
   980	              );
   981	            }
   982	          }
   983	        }
   984	
   985	        if (heroActions && socialButton) {
   986	          const heroActionsWidth = heroActions.getBoundingClientRect().width;
   987	          const socialButtonHeight =
   988	            socialButton.getBoundingClientRect().height;
   989	
   990	          if (heroCopy && heroActionsWidth > 0) {
   991	            heroCopy.style.setProperty(
   992	              "--hero-text-stack-width",
   993	              `${heroActionsWidth}px`,
   994	            );
   995	          }
   996	
   997	          if (socialButtonHeight > 0) {
   998	            const setPx = (name: string, value: number) => {
   999	              heroActions.style.setProperty(name, `${value}px`);
  1000	            };
  1001	
  1002	            setPx("--hero-social-button-height", socialButtonHeight);
  1003	            setPx("--hero-social-font-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.295833);
  1004	            setPx("--hero-social-icon-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.672);
  1005	            const baseSocialButtonHeight = 48;
  1006	            const baseSocialGap = baseSocialButtonHeight * 0.045833 + 1;
  1007	            const stretchedSocialGap =
  1008	              Math.max(0, socialButtonHeight - baseSocialButtonHeight) *
  1009	              0.091666;
  1010	
  1011	            setPx("--hero-social-gap", baseSocialGap + stretchedSocialGap);
  1012	            setPx("--hero-social-pad-x", socialButtonHeight / 8);
  1013	            setPx("--hero-view-label-font-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.275);
  1014	            heroCopy?.style.setProperty(
  1015	              "--hero-eyebrow-font-size",
  1016	              `${socialButtonHeight * 0.367}px`,
  1017	            );
  1018	            heroCopy?.style.setProperty(
  1019	              "--hero-lede-font-size",
  1020	              `${socialButtonHeight * 0.5}px`,
  1021	            );
  1022	            setPx(
  1023	              "--hero-carousel-base-font-size",
  1024	              socialButtonHeight * 0.34375,
  1025	            );
  1026	            setPx(
  1027	              "--hero-carousel-name-font-size",
  1028	              socialButtonHeight * 0.352917,
  1029	            );
  1030	            setPx(
  1031	              "--hero-carousel-category-font-size",
  1032	              socialButtonHeight * 0.176458,
  1033	            );
  1034	            setPx(
  1035	              "--hero-carousel-arrow-font-size",
  1036	              socialButtonHeight * 0.458333,
  1037	            );
  1038	          }
  1039	        }
  1040	
  1041	        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("hero-stack-sync"));
  1042	
  1043	        const viewLabel =
  1044	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-view-label");
  1045	        const githubText =
  1046	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".github-label-text");
  1047	        const githubIcon =
  1048	          document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".github-icon-mark");
  1049	
  1050	        if (viewLabel && githubText && githubIcon) {
  1051	          const viewLabelRect = viewLabel.getBoundingClientRect();
  1052	          const githubTextRect = githubText.getBoundingClientRect();
  1053	          const githubIconRect = githubIcon.getBoundingClientRect();
  1054	          const clusterLeft = Math.min(githubTextRect.left, githubIconRect.left);
  1055	          const stackLeft = clusterLeft - viewLabelRect.left;
  1056	
  1057	          viewLabel.style.setProperty(
  1058	            "--hero-view-stack-left",
  1059	            `${stackLeft}px`,
  1060	          );
  1061	        }
  1062	      });
  1063	    };
  1064	
  1065	    syncHeroActionGeometry();
  1066	    window.addEventListener("resize", syncHeroActionGeometry);
  1067	
  1068	    const targetIcon =
  1069	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".dribbble-icon-mark") ??
  1070	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".linkedin-icon-mark");
  1071	    const navBar = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".nav-bar");
  1072	    const heroVisual = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-visual");
  1073	    const heroActions = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-actions");
  1074	    const stackRow = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".stack-row");
  1075	    const proofPanel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".proof-panel");
  1076	    const profilePhoto = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".profile-photo");
  1077	    const targetButton = targetIcon?.closest(".hero-social-button");
  1078	    const viewLabel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-view-label");
  1079	    const githubText = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".github-label-text");
  1080	    const githubIcon =
  1081	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".github-icon-mark");
  1082	    const githubButton = githubText?.closest(".github-button");
  1083	    const resizeObserver =
  1084	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
  1085	        ? null
  1086	        : new ResizeObserver(syncHeroActionGeometry);
  1087	
  1088	    if (resizeObserver) {
  1089	      if (projectButtonRef.current) {
  1090	        resizeObserver.observe(projectButtonRef.current);
  1091	      }
  1092	
  1093	      if (heroActions) {
  1094	        resizeObserver.observe(heroActions);
  1095	      }
  1096	
  1097	      if (navBar) {
  1098	        resizeObserver.observe(navBar);
  1099	      }
  1100	
  1101	      if (heroVisual) {
  1102	        resizeObserver.observe(heroVisual);
  1103	      }
  1104	
  1105	      if (stackRow) {
  1106	        resizeObserver.observe(stackRow);
  1107	      }
  1108	
  1109	      if (proofPanel) {
  1110	        resizeObserver.observe(proofPanel);
  1111	      }
  1112	
  1113	      if (profilePhoto) {
  1114	        resizeObserver.observe(profilePhoto);
  1115	      }
  1116	
  1117	      if (targetIcon) {
  1118	        resizeObserver.observe(targetIcon);
  1119	      }
  1120	
  1121	      if (targetButton) {
  1122	        resizeObserver.observe(targetButton);
  1123	      }
  1124	
  1125	      if (viewLabel) {
  1126	        resizeObserver.observe(viewLabel);
  1127	      }
  1128	
  1129	      if (githubText) {
  1130	        resizeObserver.observe(githubText);
  1131	      }
  1132	
  1133	      if (githubIcon) {
  1134	        resizeObserver.observe(githubIcon);
  1135	      }
  1136	
  1137	      if (githubButton) {
  1138	        resizeObserver.observe(githubButton);
  1139	      }
  1140	    }
  1141	
  1142	    if ("fonts" in document) {
  1143	      document.fonts.ready.then(syncHeroActionGeometry).catch(() => {});
  1144	    }
  1145	
  1146	    return () => {
  1147	      if (frame) {
  1148	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  1149	      }
  1150	
  1151	      window.removeEventListener("resize", syncHeroActionGeometry);
  1152	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
  1153	    };
  1154	  }, []);
  1155	
  1156	  return (
  1157	    <span className="carousel-button-group">
  1158	      <a
  1159	        aria-label={`Open ${activeProject.name} project`}
  1160	        className="button button-primary carousel-button"
  1161	        href={activeProject.href}
  1162	        ref={projectButtonRef}
  1163	        rel="noreferrer"
  1164	        target="_blank"
  1165	      >
  1166	        <span className="carousel-button-window" aria-live="polite">
  1167	          <span className="carousel-button-label" key={activeProject.href}>
  1168	            <span
  1169	              className={`carousel-button-action carousel-button-action-${activeProject.tone}`}
  1170	              style={{
  1171	                ...strokedWhiteTextStyle,
  1172	                alignItems: "center",
  1173	                color: projectNameColors[activeProject.tone],
  1174	                display: "inline-flex",
  1175	                flexDirection: "column",
  1176	                fontSize: "var(--hero-carousel-name-font-size)",
  1177	                gap: "2.2px",
  1178	                justifyContent: "center",
  1179	                lineHeight: 0.95,
  1180	              }}
  1181	            >
  1182	              <span
  1183	                className="carousel-button-action-name"
  1184	                style={{ fontSize: activeProject.nameFontSize }}
  1185	              >
  1186	                {activeProject.name}
  1187	              </span>
  1188	              <span
  1189	                className="carousel-button-action-category"
  1190	                style={{
  1191	                  WebkitTextStroke: 0,
  1192	                  color: "#000",
  1193	                  fontSize: "var(--hero-carousel-category-font-size)",
  1194	                  fontWeight: 900,
  1195	                  letterSpacing: "0.06em",
  1196	                  lineHeight: 1,
  1197	                  textAlign: "center",
  1198	                  textShadow: "none",
  1199	                }}
  1200	              >
  1201	                {activeProject.category}
  1202	              </span>
  1203	            </span>
  1204	            <ProjectDeviceStack
  1205	              color={projectNameColors[activeProject.tone]}
  1206	              hasTablet={activeProject.hasTablet}
  1207	              hasWeb={activeProject.hasWeb}
  1208	              mobilePlatforms={activeProject.mobilePlatforms}
  1209	            />
  1210	          </span>
  1211	        </span>
  1212	      </a>
  1213	      <a
  1214	        aria-label={`Scroll to ${activeProject.name} case study`}
  1215	        className="button button-primary carousel-arrow-button"
  1216	        href={`#${activeProject.caseStudyId}`}
  1217	        onClick={handleArrowClick}
  1218	      >
  1219	        <span aria-hidden className="carousel-button-arrow">
  1220	          <CarouselArrowMark />
  1221	        </span>
  1222	      </a>
  1223	    </span>
  1224	  );
  1225	}

    32	.pnpm-store/
    33	.yarn/
    34	
    35	# Deployment archives and generated bundles
    36	*.zip
    37	dist/
    38	build/
    39	
    40	# OS/editor files
    41	.DS_Store
    42	.DS_store
    43	.idea/

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Prahl.dev Portfolio Site
     2	
     3	Next.js + Tailwind CSS portfolio start page for Prahl.dev.
     4	
     5	## What is included
     6	
     7	- `app/page.tsx` - the homepage/start page.
     8	- `app/globals.css` - the green/black visual system and responsive layout.
     9	- `public/images/profile-placeholder.png` - generated AI profile placeholder; replace this with your own square photo later using the same filename.
    10	- `public/images/start-page-mockup-reference.png` - the original generated mockup reference.
    11	- `public/favicon.svg` - simple green/black monogram favicon.
    12	
    13	## Local development
    14	
    15	```sh
    16	npm install
    17	npm run dev
    18	```
    19	
    20	Open `http://localhost:3000`.
    21	
    22	## Production build
    23	
    24	For Vercel, Netlify, or another Next.js host:
    25	
    26	```sh
    27	npm run build
    28	```
    29	
    30	For a simple static host:
    31	
    32	```sh
    33	npm run build:static
    34	```
    35	
    36	Then upload the generated `out/` folder.
    37	
    38	## Replace the profile photo
    39	
    40	Put your real square profile photo at:
    41	
    42	```txt
    43	public/images/profile-placeholder.png
    44	```
    45	
    46	Keep the filename the same and rebuild.


============================================================
FILE: DEPLOYMENT.md
============================================================

     1	# Deployment Notes
     2	
     3	## Best Option: Vercel
     4	
     5	1. Upload this `portfolio-site` folder to a GitHub repo.
     6	2. Import the repo in Vercel.
     7	3. Use the default Next.js settings.
     8	4. Build command: `npm run build`.
     9	
    10	## Netlify
    11	
    12	1. Upload this folder to a GitHub repo.
    13	2. Connect the repo in Netlify.
    14	3. Build command: `npm run build`.
    15	4. Publish directory: `.next`.
    16	5. Enable Netlify's Next.js runtime if prompted.
    17	
    18	## Simple Static Hosting
    19	
    20	Use this when the host only accepts HTML/CSS/JS files:
    21	
    22	```sh
    23	npm install
    24	npm run build:static
    25	```
    26	
    27	Upload the contents of:
    28	
    29	```txt
    30	out/
    31	```
    32	
    33	This mode does not need a running Node.js server after upload.
    34	
    35	## Contact Button
    36	
    37	The contact button currently uses:
    38	
    39	```txt
    40	mailto:hello@example.com
    41	```
    42	
    43	Replace `hello@example.com` in `app/page.tsx` with your real email address.

FILE: .github/workflows/hostinger-static-export.yml
============================================================

     1	name: Build static export for Hostinger
     2	
     3	on:
     4	  push:
     5	    branches: [main]
     6	  workflow_dispatch:
     7	
     8	permissions:
     9	  contents: write
    10	
    11	concurrency:
    12	  group: hostinger-static-export
    13	  cancel-in-progress: true
    14	
    15	jobs:
    16	  build-and-publish:
    17	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    18	    steps:
    19	      - name: Check out source
    20	        uses: actions/checkout@v4
    21	
    22	      - name: Set up Node
    23	        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
    24	        with:
    25	          node-version: 22
    26	          cache: npm
    27	
    28	      - name: Install dependencies
    29	        run: npm ci
    30	
    31	      - name: Build static export
    32	        run: npm run build:static
    33	
    34	      - name: Publish static export branch
    35	        env:
    36	          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
    37	        run: |
    38	          cd out
    39	          git init -b hostinger-deploy
    40	          git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
    41	          git config user.email "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"
    42	          git add -A
    43	          git commit -m "Deploy static export from ${GITHUB_SHA}"
    44	          git remote add origin "https://x-access-token:${GITHUB_TOKEN}@github.com/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}.git"
    45	          git push --force origin hostinger-deploy

FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/portfolio_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Portfolio project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Portfolio Next.js PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you do not understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	10. For this Portfolio repo/website project, always ask me for the commit message before committing or pushing.
    28	
    29	
    30	============================================================
    31	PORTFOLIO PROJECT NOTES
    32	============================================================
    33	
    34	- Main source branch: main
    35	- Deployment source: GitHub Actions builds main into the hostinger-deploy branch
    36	- Hostinger root directory: public_html
    37	- Main stack represented by the website: React / React Native, Next.js, Tailwind CSS, Node.js, Kotlin
    38	- Public website assets live under public/
    39	- Run this script before starting a Portfolio session to refresh the project snapshot.
    40	
    41	
    42	============================================================
    43	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    44	============================================================
    45	
    46	This file is rebuilt from the current Portfolio workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    47	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    48	
    49	The snapshot intentionally ignores generated/cache/runtime/private files:
    50	- .git
    51	- node_modules
    52	- .next
    53	- .next.*
    54	- out
    55	- dist
    56	- build
    57	- .cache
    58	- .turbo
    59	- .npm
    60	- .pnpm-store
    61	- .yarn
    62	- .DS_Store
    63	- next-env.d.ts
    64	- *.tsbuildinfo
    65	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    66	- *.zip
    67	- .env and .env*.local
    68	
    69	Binary image, PDF, and archive files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    70	
    71	
    72	============================================================
    73	PROJECT FILE TREE
    74	============================================================
    75	
    76	EOF
    77	}
    78	
    79	print_file() {
    80	  local file="$1"
    81	
    82	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    83	    {
    84	      echo ""
    85	      echo ""
    86	      echo "============================================================"
    87	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    88	      echo "============================================================"
    89	      echo ""
    90	      nl -ba "$file"
    91	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    92	  fi
    93	}
    94	
    95	append_project_tree() {
    96	  # Use Git's own ignore rules so the snapshot cannot drift from .gitignore.
    97	  # Include tracked files plus untracked files that Git does not ignore.
    98	  printf '%s\n' "$PROJECT_DIR" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    99	
   100	  git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" ls-files --cached --others --exclude-standard \
   101	    | sort \
   102	    | while IFS= read -r relative_file; do
   103	      case "$relative_file" in
   104	        .git/*|node_modules/*|.next/*|.next.*/*|out/*|dist/*|build/*|.cache/*|.turbo/*|.npm/*|.pnpm-store/*|.yarn/*|.DS_Store|*/.DS_Store|*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt|*.zip|.env|.env*.local|next-env.d.ts|*.tsbuildinfo)
   105	          continue
   106	          ;;
   107	      esac
   108	
   109	      printf '%s/%s\n' "$PROJECT_DIR" "$relative_file" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   110	    done
   111	}
   112	
   113	print_inventory_header() {
   114	  local title="$1"
   115	
   116	  {
   117	    echo ""
   118	    echo ""
   119	    echo "============================================================"
   120	    echo "$title"
   121	    echo "============================================================"
   122	    echo ""
   123	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   124	}
   125	
   126	print_file_inventory_line() {
   127	  local file="$1"
   128	  local size
   129	  local modified
   130	
   131	  size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   132	  modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   133	  printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   134	}
   135	
   136	print_dir_inventory() {
   137	  local title="$1"
   138	  local dir="$2"
   139	
   140	  print_inventory_header "$title"
   141	
   142	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
   143	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   144	    return
   145	  fi
   146	
   147	  find "$dir" -type f \
   148	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
   149	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
   150	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   151	    print_file_inventory_line "$file"
   152	  done
   153	}
   154	
   155	print_matching_inventory() {
   156	  local title="$1"
   157	  shift
   158	
   159	  print_inventory_header "$title"
   160	
   161	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" "$@" -type f \
   162	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git/*" \
   163	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/node_modules/*" \
   164	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.next/*" \
   165	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.next.*/*" \
   166	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/out/*" \
   167	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
   168	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
   169	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   170	    print_file_inventory_line "$file"
   171	  done
   172	}
   173	
   174	append_git_context() {
   175	  {
   176	    echo ""
   177	    echo ""
   178	    echo "============================================================"
   179	    echo "GIT CONTEXT"
   180	    echo "============================================================"
   181	    echo ""
   182	    echo "Current branch:"
   183	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" branch --show-current 2>/dev/null || true
   184	    echo ""
   185	    echo "Status:"
   186	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" status --short --branch 2>/dev/null || true
   187	    echo ""
   188	    echo "Recent commits:"
   189	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" log --oneline --decorate -10 2>/dev/null || true
   190	    echo ""
   191	    echo "Remotes:"
   192	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" remote -v 2>/dev/null || true
   193	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   194	}
   195	
   196	write_snapshot() {
   197	  write_header
   198	
   199	  append_project_tree
   200	
   201	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   202	
   203	
   204	============================================================
   205	FILE CONTENTS - APP SOURCE
   206	============================================================
   207	
   208	EOF
   209	
   210	  for file in \
   211	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/layout.tsx" \
   212	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/page.tsx" \
   213	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/ProjectCarouselButton.tsx" \
   214	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/globals.css"
   215	  do
   216	    print_file "$file"
   217	  done
   218	
   219	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   220	
   221	
   222	============================================================
   223	FILE CONTENTS - ROOT / TOOLING / DEPLOYMENT
   224	============================================================
   225	
   226	EOF
   227	
   228	  for file in \
   229	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.gitignore" \
   230	    "$PROJECT_DIR/README.md" \
   231	    "$PROJECT_DIR/DEPLOYMENT.md" \
   232	    "$PROJECT_DIR/package.json" \
   233	    "$PROJECT_DIR/package-lock.json" \
   234	    "$PROJECT_DIR/next.config.ts" \
   235	    "$PROJECT_DIR/postcss.config.mjs" \
   236	    "$PROJECT_DIR/tsconfig.json" \
   237	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.github/workflows/hostinger-static-export.yml" \
   238	    "$PROJECT_DIR/public/favicon.svg" \
   239	    "$PROJECT_DIR/read_project.sh"
   240	  do
   241	    print_file "$file"
   242	  done
   243	
   244	  append_git_context
   245	
   246	  print_dir_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY - PUBLIC IMAGES" "$PROJECT_DIR/public/images"
   247	  print_matching_inventory "PDF WORKLOG INVENTORY" \( -name "portfolio_worklog_*.pdf" \)
   248	  print_matching_inventory "DEPLOYMENT ARCHIVE INVENTORY" \( -name "*.zip" \)
   249	
   250	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   251	
   252	
   253	============================================================
   254	NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
   255	============================================================
   256	
   257	The Portfolio snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private paths:
   258	- .git
   259	- node_modules
   260	- .next
   261	- .next.*
   262	- out
   263	- dist
   264	- build
   265	- .cache
   266	- .turbo
   267	- .npm
   268	- .pnpm-store
   269	- .yarn
   270	- .DS_Store
   271	- next-env.d.ts
   272	- *.tsbuildinfo
   273	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
   274	- *.zip
   275	- .env and .env*.local
   276	
   277	The snapshot lists public images, PDF worklogs, and deployment archives by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF/ZIP bytes.
   278	
   279	EOF
   280	}
   281	
   282	write_snapshot
   283	
   284	echo "Done."
   285	echo ""
   286	echo "Snapshot created here:"
   287	echo "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   288	echo ""
   289	echo "Open it with:"
   290	echo "open \"$OUTPUT_FILE\""

============================================================
NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
============================================================

The Portfolio snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private paths:
- .git
- node_modules
- .next
- .next.*
- out
- dist
- build
- .cache
- .turbo
- .npm
- .pnpm-store
- .yarn
- .DS_Store
- next-env.d.ts
- *.tsbuildinfo
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.zip
- .env and .env*.local

The snapshot lists public images, PDF worklogs, and deployment archives by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF/ZIP bytes.
